## 添加拓撲規則 (資料管理工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Add Rule To Topology

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 拓撲工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-rule-to-topology.html

**id**　b9e1fbb8f92ff7a159-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-rule-to-topology.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/add-rule-to-topology.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_topology", "label_zh": "輸入拓撲", "label_en": "Input Topology", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Topology Layer", "description_en": "The topology to which the new rule will be added."}, {"parameter": "rule_type", "label_zh": "規則類型", "label_en": "Rule Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the topology rule that will be added. Must Not Have Gaps (Area)—There must be no voids within a single polygon or between adjacent polygons. All polygons must form a continuous surface. An error will always exist on the perimeter of the surface. You can either ignore this error or mark it as an exception. Use this rule for data that must completely cover an area. For example, soil polygons cannot include gaps or form voids; they must cover an entire area. Must Not Overlap (Area)—The interior of polygons must not overlap. The polygons can share edges or vertices. This rule is used when an area cannot belong to two or more polygons. It is useful for modeling administrative boundaries, such as ZIP Codes or voting districts, and mutually exclusive area classifications, such as land cover or landform type. Must Be Covered By Feature Class Of (Area-Area)—A polygon in one feature class (or subtype) must share all of its area with polygons in another feature class (or subtype). An area in the first feature class that is not covered by polygons from the other feature class is an error. This rule is used when an area of one type, such as a state, should be completely covered by areas of another type, such as counties. Must Cover Each Other (Area-Area)—The polygons of one feature class (or subtype) must share all of their area with the polygons of another feature class (or subtype). Polygons may share edges or vertices. Any area defined in either feature class that is not shared with the other is an error. This rule is used when two systems of classification are used for the same geographic area, and any given point defined in one system must also be defined in the other. One such case occurs with nested hierarchical datasets, such as census blocks and block groups or small watersheds and large drainage basins. The rule can also be applied to nonhierarchically related polygon feature classes, such as soil type and slope class. Must Be Covered By (Area-Area)—The polygons of one feature class (or subtype) must be contained within polygons of another feature class (or subtype). Polygons may share edges or vertices. Any area defined in the contained feature class must be covered by an area in the covering feature class. This rule is used when area features of a given type must be located within features of another type. This rule is useful when modeling areas that are subsets of a larger surrounding area, such as management units within forests or blocks within block groups. Must Not Overlap With (Area-Area)—The interior of polygons in one feature class (or subtype) must not overlap with the interior of polygons in another feature class (or subtype). Polygons of the two feature classes can share edges or vertices or be completely disjointed. This rule is used when an area cannot belong to two separate feature classes. It is useful for combining two mutually exclusive systems of area classification, such as zoning and water body type, in which areas defined within the zoning class cannot also be defined in the water body class and vice versa. Must Be Covered By Boundary Of (Line-Area)—Lines must be covered by the boundaries of area features. This is useful for modeling lines, such as lot lines, that must coincide with the edge of polygon features, such as lots. Must Be Covered By Boundary Of (Point-Area)—Points must fall on the boundaries of area features. This is useful when the point features help support the boundary system, such as boundary markers, which must be found on the edges of certain areas. Must Be Properly Inside (Point-Area)—Points must fall within area features. This is useful when the point features are related to polygons, such as wells and well pads or address points and parcels. Must Not Overlap (Line)—Lines must not overlap with lines in the same feature class (or subtype). This rule is used when line segments should not be duplicated, for example, in a stream feature class. Lines can cross or intersect but cannot share segments. Must Not Intersect (Line)—Line features from the same feature class (or subtype) must not cross or overlap each other. Lines can share endpoints. This rule is used for contour lines that should never cross each other or when the intersection of lines should only occur at endpoints, such as street segments and intersections. Must Not Have Dangles (Line)—A line feature must touch lines from the same feature class (or subtype) at both endpoints. An endpoint that is not connected to another line is called a dangle. This rule is used when line features must form closed loops, such as when they are defining the boundaries of polygon features. It can also be used when lines typically connect to other lines, as with streets. In this case, exceptions can be used when the rule is occasionally violated, as with cul-de-sac or dead-end street segments. Must Not Have Pseudo-Nodes (Line)—A line must connect to at least two other lines at each endpoint. Lines that connect to one other line (or to themselves) are considered to have pseudo nodes. This rule is used when line features must form closed loops, such as when they define the boundaries of polygons or when line features logically must connect to two other line features at each end, as with segments in a stream network, with exceptions being marked for the originating ends of first-order streams. Must Be Covered By Feature Class Of (Line-Line)—Lines from one feature class (or subtype) must be covered by the lines in another feature class (or subtype). This is useful for modeling logically different but spatially coincident lines, such as routes and streets. For example, a bus route feature class must not depart from the streets defined in the street feature class. Must Not Overlap With (Line-Line)—A line from one feature class (or subtype) must not overlap with line features in another feature class (or subtype). This rule is used when line features cannot share the same space. For example, roads must not overlap with railroads, or depression subtypes of contour lines cannot overlap with other contour lines. Must Be Covered By (Point-Line)—Points in one feature class must be covered by lines in another feature class. It does not constrain the covering portion of the line to be an endpoint. This rule is useful for points that fall along a set of lines, such as highway signs along highways. Must Be Covered By Endpoint Of (Point-Line)—Points in one feature class must be covered by the endpoints of lines in another feature class. This rule is similar to the Endpoint Must Be Covered By line rule except that, in cases where the rule is violated, it is the point feature that is marked as an error rather than the line. Boundary corner markers may be constrained to be covered by the endpoints of boundary lines. Boundary Must Be Covered By (Area-Line)—Boundaries of polygon features must be covered by lines in another feature class. This rule is used when area features must have line features that mark the boundaries of the areas. This is usually when the areas have one set of attributes and their boundaries have other attributes. For example, parcels are stored in the geodatabase along with their boundaries. Each parcel is defined by one or more line features that store information about their length or the date surveyed, and every parcel must exactly match its boundaries. Boundary Must Be Covered By Boundary Of (Area-Area)—Boundaries of polygon features in one feature class (or subtype) must be covered by boundaries of polygon features in another feature class (or subtype). This is useful when polygon features in one feature class, such as subdivisions, are composed of multiple polygons in another class, such as parcels, and the shared boundaries must be aligned. Must Not Self-Overlap (Line)—Line features must not overlap themselves. They can cross or touch but must not have coincident segments. This rule is useful for features, such as streets, in which segments might touch in a loop but the same street must not follow the same course twice. Must Not Self-Intersect (Line)—Line features must not cross or overlap themselves. This rule is useful for lines, such as contour lines, that cannot cross themselves. Must Not Intersect Or Touch Interior (Line)—A line in one feature class (or subtype) must only touch other lines of the same feature class (or subtype) at endpoints. Any line segment in which features overlap or any intersection that is not at an endpoint is an error. This rule is useful when lines must only be connected at endpoints, such as lot lines, which must split (only connect to the endpoints of) back lot lines and cannot overlap each other. Endpoint Must Be Covered By (Line-Point)—The endpoints of line features must be covered by point features in another feature class. This is useful for modeling cases in which a fitting must connect two pipes, or a street intersection must be found at the junction of two streets. Contains Point (Area-Point)—A polygon in one feature class must contain at least one point from another feature class. Points must be within the polygon, not on the boundary. This is useful when every polygon must have at least one associated point, such as when parcels must have an address point. Must Be Single Part (Line)—Lines must have only one part. This rule is useful when line features, such as highways, may not have multiple parts. Must Coincide With (Point-Point)—Points in one feature class (or subtype) must be coincident with points in another feature class (or subtype). This is useful when points must be covered by other points, such as transformers that must coincide with power poles in electric distribution networks and observation points that must coincide with stations. Must Be Disjoint (Point)—Points must be separated spatially from other points in the same feature class (or subtype). Any points that overlap are errors. This is useful for ensuring that points are not coincident or duplicated in the same feature class, such as in layers of cities, parcel lot ID points, wells, or street lamp poles. Must Not Intersect With (Line-Line)—Line features from one feature class (or subtype) must not cross or overlap lines from another feature class (or subtype). Lines can share endpoints. This rule is used when lines from two layers should never cross each other or when the intersection of lines should only occur at endpoints, such as streets and railroads. Must Not Intersect or Touch Interior With (Line-Line)—A line in one feature class (or subtype) must only touch other lines of another feature class (or subtype) at endpoints. Any line segment in which features overlap or any intersection that is not at an endpoint is an error. This rule is useful when lines from two layers must only be connected at endpoints. Must Be Inside (Line-Area)—A line must be contained within the boundary of an area feature. This is useful when lines may partially or totally coincide with area boundaries but cannot extend beyond polygons, such as state highways that must be inside state borders and rivers that must be within watersheds. Contains One Point (Area-Point)—Each polygon must contain one point feature and each point feature must fall within a single polygon. This is used when there must be a one-to-one correspondence between features of a polygon feature class and features of a point feature class, such as administrative boundaries and their capital cities. Each point must be properly inside exactly one polygon and each polygon must properly contain exactly one point. Points must be within the polygon, not on the boundary."}, {"parameter": "in_featureclass", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵類", "label_en": "Input Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input or origin feature class."}, {"parameter": "subtype", "label_zh": "輸入子類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Subtype (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The subtype for the input or origin feature class. Provide the subtype name (not the code). If subtypes do not exist on the input feature class, or you want the rule to be applied to all subtypes in the feature class, leave this parameter blank."}, {"parameter": "in_featureclass2", "label_zh": "目標圖徵類 （可選）", "label_en": "Destination Feature Class (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The destination feature class for the topology rule."}, {"parameter": "subtype2", "label_zh": "目標子類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Destination Subtype (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The subtype for the destination feature class. Provide the subtype name (not the code). If subtypes do not exist on the origin feature class, or you want the rule to be applied to all subtypes in the feature class, leave this parameter blank."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.AddRuleToTopology(in_topology, rule_type, in_featureclass, {subtype}, {in_featureclass2}, {subtype2})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_topology、rule_type、in_featureclass、subtype、in_featureclass2、subtype2。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 分析資料集 (資料管理工具) · 匯總

**id**　ba0b12a72d6d073a6f-1

**english**　Analyze Datasets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html

**text**　更新基表、增量表和存檔表的資料庫統計資料，以及這些表的索引的統計資料。 此工具用于企業級地理資料庫，以便關系資料庫管理系統 (RDBMS) 的查詢優化程序獲得最佳性能。 過時的統計資料可能會影響地理資料庫的性能。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#summary

## 分析資料集 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　ba0b12a72d6d073a6f-2

**english**　Analyze Datasets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html

**text**　輸入工作空間必須為資料庫或企業級地理資料庫。 分析資料集工具不適用于文件或移動地理資料庫。
執行完資料加載、刪除、更新和壓縮操作后，在資料庫中更新 RDBMS 統計資料十分重要。
包括系統表參數用于確定狀態和狀態譜系表是否需要分析。 這些表將追蹤傳統版本。 未選中此參數時，不會分析表；選中此參數時，將分析表。
此工具不支持用于 SAP HANA。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#usage

## 分析資料集 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　ba0b12a72d6d073a6f-22

**english**　Analyze Datasets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html

**text**　# Name: AnalyzeDatasets.py
# Description: analyzes all datasets in an enterprise geodatabase
#              for a given user.

# Import system modules
import arcpy
import os

# set workspace
# the user in this workspace must be the owner of the data to analyze.
workspace = "C:\\MyProject\\MyDataConnection.sde"

# set the workspace environment
arcpy.env.workspace = workspace

# NOTE: Analyze Datasets can accept a Python list of datasets.

# Get the user name for the workspace
userName = arcpy.Describe(workspace).connectionProperties.user

# Get a list of all the datasets the user owns by using a wildcard that incldues the user name
# First, get all the stand alone tables, feature classes and rasters.
dataList = arcpy.ListTables(userName + "*") + arcpy.ListFeatureClasses(userName + "*") + arcpy.ListRasters(userName + "*")

# Next, for feature datasets get all of the datasets and featureclasses
# from the list and add them to the master list.
for dataset in arcpy.ListDatasets(userName + "*", "Feature"):
    arcpy.env.workspace = os.path.join(workspace,dataset)
    dataList += arcpy.ListFeatureClasses(userName + "*") + arcpy.ListDatasets(userName + "*")

# reset the workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = workspace

# Run analyze datasets
# Note: to use the "SYSTEM" option the workspace user must be an administrator.
arcpy.management.AnalyzeDatasets(workspace, "NO_SYSTEM", dataList, "ANALYZE_BASE", "ANALYZE_DELTA", "ANALYZE_ARCHIVE")
print("Analyze Complete")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#derived-output

## 分析資料集 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**english**　Analyze Datasets

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html

**id**　ba0b12a72d6d073a6f-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Analyze Datasets，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料庫連接 [input_database]、包括系統表 [include_system]、待分析資料集 （可選） [in_datasets]、分析所選資料集的基表 （可選） [analyze_base]、分析所選資料集的增量表 （可選） [analyze_delta]、分析所選資料集的存檔表 （可選） [analyze_archive]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/analyze-datasets.html#parameters

Note: You must be the geodatabase administrator to use this parameter. This parameter only applies to geodatabases. If the input workspace is a database, this parameter will be ignored. Checked—Statistics will be gathered on the states and state lineages tables. Unchecked—Statistics will not be gathered on the states and state lineages tables. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "in_datasets", "label_zh": "待分析資料集 （可選）", "label_en": "Datasets to Analyze (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The names of the datasets that will be analyzed. Only datasets that are owned by the connected user will be displayed."}, {"parameter": "analyze_base", "label_zh": "分析所選資料集的基表 （可選）", "label_en": "Analyze Base Tables for Selected Dataset(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the selected dataset base tables will be analyzed. This parameter only applies to geodatabases. If the input workspace is a database, this parameter will be ignored. Checked—Statistics will be gathered for the base tables for the selected datasets. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will not be gathered for the base tables for the selected datasets."}, {"parameter": "analyze_delta", "label_zh": "分析所選資料集的增量表 （可選）", "label_en": "Analyze Delta Tables for Selected Dataset(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the selected dataset delta tables will be analyzed. This parameter only applies to geodatabases that contain traditional versions. If the input workspace is a database or does not participate in traditional versioning, this parameter will be ignored. Checked—Statistics will be gathered for the delta tables for the selected datasets. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will not be gathered for the delta tables for the selected datasets."}, {"parameter": "analyze_archive", "label_zh": "分析所選資料集的存檔表 （可選）", "label_en": "Analyze Archive Tables for Selected Dataset(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the selected dataset archive tables will be analyzed. This parameter only applies to geodatabases that contain archive-enabled datasets. If the input workspace is a database, this parameter will be ignored. Checked—Statistics will be gathered for the archive tables for the selected datasets. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will not be gathered for the archive tables for the selected datasets."}]

## 160516：未在出行模式的輸入數組中找到默認的出行模式名稱。 · 注:

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 在相交處設置控制點 (制圖工具) · 匯總

**id**　ba3572b018713a9096-1

**english**　Set Control Point At Intersect

**chapter**

**kind**　說明

**text**　此工具可在由一個或多個線圖徵或面圖徵共用的折點處創建控制點。 此工具通常用于同步相鄰面上的邊界符號系統。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#summary

## 在相交處設置控制點 (制圖工具) · 注意：

**id**　ba3572b018713a9096-2

**english**　Set Control Point At Intersect

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html

**text**　此工具會修改輸入資料。 有關詳細信息以及避免意外更改資料的策略，請參閱修改或更新輸入資料的工具。
控制點可用于控制圖案化符號系統（如虛線）的相位調整。 使用此工具可同步相鄰面上經過相位調整的輪廓符號系統。
輸入圖徵參數值必須為線或面圖徵類。 輸入次要圖徵參數值可以為點、線或面圖徵類。 次要圖徵提供可供與輸入圖徵進行比較的幾何。
僅會修改輸入圖徵參數值。 輸入次要圖徵參數值將用于確定控制點在輸入圖徵參數值上的放置位置。
若要沿兩個圖徵類對齊虛線，需運行該工具兩次：每次將每個圖徵類作為輸入圖徵和輸入第二個圖徵參數值。 這將在每個圖徵類上放置匹配的控制點，這些控制點用于對齊虛線。
不會移除現有控制點。
如果只輸入一個圖層，則只會在三條線段的交點處創建控制點。 如果輸入次要圖徵，則將在線段相交處添加控制點。
兩個輸入圖層均可接受多部件幾何。
無法向 shapefile 添加控制點。
處理大型資料集可能會超出內存限制。 在這種情況下，考慮通過在制圖分區 環境中確定一個相關的面圖徵類來通過分區處理輸入資料。 將按順序處理分區邊界定義的資料部分。 結果無縫，而且在分區邊界一致。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#usage

## 在相交處設置控制點 (制圖工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　ba3572b018713a9096-14

**english**　Set Control Point At Intersect

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html

**text**　# Name: SetControlPointAtIntersect_standalone_script.py
# Description: Creates a control point at vertices that are shared by one or
#              more line or polygon features.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"
arcpy.env.cartographicPartitions = "partitions.lyrx"

# Set local variables
in_line_or_polygon_features = "parcels.lyrx"
in_features = "roads.lyrx"

# Run Set Representation Control Point At Intersect
arcpy.cartography.SetControlPointAtIntersect(in_line_or_polygon_features,
                                             in_features)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#derived-output

## 在相交處設置控制點 (制圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**english**　Set Control Point At Intersect

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html

**id**　ba3572b018713a9096-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Set Control Point At Intersect，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_line_or_polygon_features]、輸入第二個圖徵 （可選） [in_features]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/set-control-point-at-intersect.html#parameters

## 水文分析應用示例 · 水文分析應用示例

**id**　ba63dd8e08709f501c-1

**english**　Hydrologic analysis sample applications

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集
- 水文分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

**text**　需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
水文建模工具提供了描述表面物理組件的方法。 利用水文工具，可以識別匯、確定流向、計算流量、描繪集水區以及創建河流網絡。 下圖是從高程模型派生的結果河流網絡：
使用高程網格或數字高程模型 (DEM) 作為輸入，可以自動描繪排水系統并量化系統的特征。 下圖說明了從 DEM 計算集水區和河流網絡所涉及的步驟。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

## 水文分析應用示例 · 水文分析應用示例

**id**　ba63dd8e08709f501c-2

**english**　Hydrologic analysis sample applications

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集
- 水文分析工具集概念

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

**steps**　
1. 將在其上執行水文分析的 DEM。 輸入 DEM 表面網格
2. 使用 DEM 作為流向工具的輸入，可以確定水流出每個像元的方向。 確定流向。
3. 使用匯工具，可以識別原始 DEM 中的所有匯。 匯通常是一個低于其周圍環境值的不正確值。 上圖中顯示的洼地（分散彩色點）存在問題，因為任何流入其中的水都無法流出。 為確保正確繪制排水區，可以使用填洼工具填充這些洼地。 識別匯。
4. 使用集水區工具，可以為指定位置描繪集水區。 但是，如果您只希望計算河流網絡，則可以忽略此步驟。 描繪集水區。
5. 要創建河流網絡，請使用流量工具計算流向某個位置的上坡像元數。 在上一步中創建的輸出流向網格將用作輸入。 流量輸出
6. 可以在從流量工具派生的網格上指定閾值；初始階段是定義河流網絡系統。 可以使用條件函數工具和一個表達式來完成此任務，此表達式可將輸入值中高于閾值的所有像元設置為值 1。 在本示例中，表達式將定義流入其中的像元數量超過 100 的所有像元作為河流網絡的一部分，而少于該閾值的像元將為 NoData。
7. 要表示網絡中每個河段的級別，請應用河網分級工具。 可用的分級方法包括 Shreve 和 Strahler 技術。 河網分級輸出
8. 使用水流長度工具，可以確定給定集水區內每個像元的水流路徑長度（上坡或下坡）。 這對于計算水流流經集水區的時間非常有用。 水流長度輸出

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/hydrologic-analysis-sample-applications.html

## 110682: 沒有創建 SHAP 值摘要繪圖, 因為輸入模型文件只包含明確的解釋變量。 · 說明說明

**id**　ba693c8b93f79bdbce-1

**english**　110682: The SHAP Value Summary Plot was not created because the input model file only contains categorical explanatory variables.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110601-110700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110676-110700-110682.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110676-110700-110682.html

**text**　要創建 SHAP 值摘要繪圖圖表, 至少必須連續使用一個解釋變量。 所有提供的解釋變量都是絕對的, 因此沒有創建該圖表 。 或者, 請使用至少包含一個持續解釋變量的輸入模型文件 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "To create the SHAP Values Summary Plot chart, at least one explanatory variable must be continuous. All provided explanatory variables were categorical, so the chart was not created. Alternatively, use an input model file that includes at least one continuous explanatory variable.", "section": "Description"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110676-110700-110682.html#description

## 網格分析工具箱概述 · 網格分析工具箱概述

**id**　ba8792c705785c6f15-1

**english**　An overview of the Raster Analysis toolbox

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網格分析工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

**text**　“網格分析”工具箱包含一套在門戶中對資料進行網格分析的工具。 通過將處理任務分配到多個服務器節點，相比于臺式計算機，可在更短的時間內處理大型資料集。 網格分析工具由 ArcGIS Image Server 提供支持。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

## 網格分析工具箱概述 · 注:

**id**　ba8792c705785c6f15-2

**english**　An overview of the Raster Analysis toolbox

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網格分析工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

**text**　如果您登錄到具有針對網格分析進行了配置 ArcGIS Image Server 的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶，則網格分析門戶工具可用。 當工具運行時，ArcGIS Pro 將充當客戶端，并且處理將在與 ArcGIS Enterprise 聯合的服務器中進行。 門戶工具接受來自門戶的圖層作為輸入，并在門戶中創建輸出。
如果活動門戶為 ArcGIS Online，則工具將不可用。
要訪問這些工具，可通過分析功能區或地理處理窗格中的門戶選項卡。
了解有關門戶工具的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/raster-analysis/an-overview-of-the-raster-analysis-toolbox.html

## 160805：出行模式引用了不存在的時間屬性。 · 注:

**id**　bac6a88a83e757c9da-2

**english**　160805: The travel mode refers to a time attribute that does not exist.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 160801-160900

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160801-160825-160805.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160801-160825-160805.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**english**　Modify LRS Event

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

**parameter**　store_route_name

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 中位數(_R)
- 確定事件記錄是否保存路線名稱。 AS_ IS - 不更改屬性。 這是默認的。 Storee_ROUTE_NAME - 將事件記錄的路徑名稱儲存在路名中。 NO_ STORE_ ROUTE_NAME - 不要將路線名稱與事件記錄一起存儲。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["store_route_name (Optional)", "Determines if the event records will store the route name. AS_IS—No change to the property. This is the default. STORE_ROUTE_NAME—Stores the route name with the event records. NO_STORE_ROUTE_NAME—Does not store the route name with the event records.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs-event.html#derived-output
